Document 016: 2008再现！一文读懂华尔街最热门交易：AI CDS

Source PDF: all report pdf/2008再现！一文读懂华尔街最热门交易：AI CDS.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

当 AI 巨头们疯狂烧钱扩张之际，信贷市场已拉响警报。博通、英伟达等芯⽚商 CDS 利差半
年内暴涨逾 170% ，⾕歌、亚⻢逊等 10 家核⼼ AI 企业虽仅占投资级指数成分 8% ，却承载了
16% 的隐含信贷⻛险。芯⽚制造商正从科技公司异变为 " 集中贷款机构 " ， CDS 交易量同⽐
激增逾 1900% 。
摘要：
巴克莱银⾏（ Barclays ）发布了⼀份针对⼈⼯智能相关企业信⽤违约互换（ CDS ）市场的综合
报告，指出随着资本⽀出的⼤幅扩张， AI 相关企业的信贷⻛险已出现显著的重新定价，其 CDS
交易量及利差均呈现急剧上升趋势。
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇，巴克莱银⾏报告指出，包括⾕歌、亚⻢逊、 Meta 、微软、甲⻣⽂等超⼤规模云计算
服务商，以及 AMD 、博通、英伟达等芯⽚制造商在内的10 家核⼼ AI 企业，⽬前仅占 CDX 投资级
指数成分的 8% ，但已占到该指数隐含信贷⻛险的 16%。
巴克莱强调，过去六个⽉，这⼗家公司的信⽤⻛险均有所扩⼤，⽬前均处于其六个⽉波动区间

的上限附近。
（ AI 群体：当前 5 年期 CDS 的六个⽉区间对⽐）
这意味着持有该投资级指数的投资者已被动承担了⼤量 AI 领域的波动⻛险。此外，这些核⼼ AI 企
业的 CDS 与现⾦债券的基差已转为正值，即购买违约保护的成本已超过持有这些企业债券所带
来的额外收益。
据彭博报道，这⼀反常现象主要由于⼤量未持有相关债券的机构（如进⾏对冲的股票基⾦和银
⾏）正⼤举买⼊ CDS ，以对冲巨⼤的 AI 资本⽀出及相关贷款⻛险。
此番技术性异动在信贷对冲与基差扭曲的逻辑上，与 2008 年当年极其相似。当年⼤量未持有次
级房贷债券的机构（如对冲基⾦、投资银⾏），出于宏观看空或⻛险规避，通过买⼊ CDS 保护来
下注违约⻛险。这种密集的 “ 裸对冲 ” 衍⽣品买盘将 CDS 利差极度推⾼，甚⾄超过了现货债券本⾝
的信⽤利差，直接推升 CDS- 现⾦债券基差转正。
在巴克莱发布该报告之际，头部科技企业的融资需求正持续向市场施压。近期，微软启动了逾
250 亿美元的债券发⾏，亚⻢逊单季度新增超 480 亿美元⻓期债务，博通也计划于明年初筹集⾼

达 210 亿美元资⾦。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这些巨额融资进⼀步推⾼了市场的对冲需求，导致相关企业 CDS 成本触及历史新⾼。
芯⽚制造商信贷特征转变，利差⼤幅⾛阔
在这波信贷重新定价中，芯⽚制造商的 CDS 利差⾛阔最为显著。
六个⽉前，博通、 AMD 和英伟达的 CDS 利差尚处于同类别的最低⽔平，在 40 个基点左右徘徊。
（ AI 群体 5 年期 CDS ，六个⽉前 vs. 现在，按六个⽉变化幅度排序）
然⽽，如今这三家公司的 CDS 利差已跃居前列，其中博通的 5 年期 CDS 在六个⽉内飙升 258% ，
AMD 上涨 177% ，英伟达上涨 172% 。相⽐之下，评级为 AAA 的微软仅上涨了 20% 。
巴克莱在报告中指出，随着芯⽚供应商开始向客⼾提供贷款以购买其芯⽚（即供应商融资），信
贷市场已开始将这些公司视为拥有⾼度集中贷款账本的贷款机构。
例如，在银⾏对 Anthropic 的 630 亿美元芯⽚交易产⽣疑虑后，博通出⾯提供了担保。基于此，巴
克莱建议买⼊博通的 5 年期 CDS 保护，作为对冲更⼴泛 AI 融资压⼒的宏观策略。

基差转正与对冲需求驱动交易量激增
CDS 与现⾦债券的基差转正，是当前 AI 信贷市场最显著的技术特征。
过去⼀年， CDX 投资级指数中⾮ AI 企业的基差基本保持负值，即 CDS 利差低于债券利差，但 AI
群体的基差已上升约 50 个基点并转为正值。在芯⽚制造商和甲⻣⽂中，这⼀正基差尤为明显。

### Page 3

（ AI 群体的 CDS- 现⾦债券基差现已转为正值，⽽⾮  AI 群体的基差仍为负值）
这种结构性变化伴随着交易量的爆炸性增⻓。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 23 ⽇，超⼤规模云服务商的 4 周平均周交
易量达到约 60 亿美元，同⽐猛增约 740% ；芯⽚制造商的周交易量达到 30 亿美元，同⽐激增
1900% 。
（超⼤规模云服务商与半导体企业的  CDS 交易量已显著增加）
未平仓合约（ Open interest ）数据同样印证了资⾦的持续流⼊。超⼤规模云服务商的净未平仓
合约名义价值已从⼀年前的约 50 亿美元翻倍⾄ 200 亿美元，芯⽚制造商则从约 19 亿美元增⾄ 86

### Page 4

亿美元。
（ AI 群体的未平仓合约已从⼀年前的约 70 亿美元增⾄⽬前的 280 亿美元以上）
这 10 家 AI 企业⽬前占美洲企业单⼀名称 CDS 所有未平仓合约的近 14% ，远⾼于⼀年前的 4% 。
曲线形态分化，交易策略凸显个股差异
通过对 CDS 曲线3 年期与 5 年期利差对⽐的分析，巴克莱指出了个别企业的定价异常。
例如，亚⻢逊与⾕歌的 5 年期 CDS ⽔平相当（ 80-81 基点），但亚⻢逊的 3s5s 曲线（ 3 年与 5 年利
差）仅为 18 基点，显得过于平坦。

### Page 5

（当前  3s5s CDS 曲线的绝对值，以及占  5 年期  CDS 的百分⽐）
巴克莱认为这可能与亚⻢逊  6 ⽉份的⼀笔定期贷款造成的买盘压⼒有关，并建议买⼊亚⻢逊 5 年
期保护或构建陡峭化交易组合。
财报数据随后印证了对冲的必要性，亚⻢逊公布第三季度⾃由现⾦流消耗达 335 亿美元，资本⽀
出⾼达 773 亿美元。
作为市场上最具流动性的 CDS 标的，甲⻣⽂的基差在样本中处于⾼位。
（当前 CDS- 现⾦债券基差在⼀年区间内所处位置对⽐图）
巴克莱建议卖出甲⻣⽂的 5 年期基差，即卖出 2032 年到期债券同时卖出 CDS 保护，以博弈基差的
收敛。此前，甲⻣⽂宣布将未来⼏年的额外融资上限设定为 200 亿美元。
此外，英特尔是样本中唯⼀基差仍为负值（ -14 基点）且曲线相对陡峭的企业，这主要由于其并
未深度参与同⾏的供应商融资模式。但在疲软的业绩指引公布后，其股价创下了⾃ 1982 年以来

### Page 6

的最⼤单⽇跌幅。
⾼收益市场缩影： CoreWeave 违约保护成本⾼企
在投资级之外， CoreWeave 作为 CDX ⾼收益指数中唯⼀直接相关的 AI 企业 CDS ，成为了观察数
据中⼼和新型云服务商信贷⻛险的独特窗⼝。
⽬前， CoreWeave 的 5 年期 CDS 已采⽤ “ 预付点数 ” （ points upfront ）的定价⽅式，达到 15.5 点，
这⼀通常⽤于受困债务的定价模式凸显了市场对其⻛险的⾼度警惕。
（ CoreWeave 5 年期 CDS ，以预付点数报价）
数据显⽰， CoreWeave 的 CDS 周交易量已从⼀年前的 3200 万美元升⾄ 3.02 亿美元，未平仓合约
增⾄ 8.5 亿美元。
（ CoreWeave 周交易量已从⼀年前的 3200 万美元升⾄  3.02 亿美元，未平仓合约增⾄ 8.5 亿美
元）

### Page 7

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 2

世界経済：AI・防衛関連需要を受け景況感は好調。一方でインフレ圧力の広がりも確認
 9月のグローバル製造業PMIは53.0（8月52.3）と堅調。非製造業は54.5（8月53.6）と40か月ぶりの高水準
― 製造業はAI・防衛関連投資需要を受け生産、新規受注ともに上昇。非製造業も幅広い業種で業況が改善
― 一方、仕入・販売価格指数はともに上昇。エネルギーや輸送価格上昇によるインフレ圧力の広がりを示唆
 国・地域別では米国・ユーロ圏が製造業・非製造業ともに改善。とりわけ米国の好調が目立つ
― 米国ではAI関連支出の拡大に伴い機械・設備投資および生産が増加。非製造業においても情報通信業が突出して好調
― ユーロ圏でも、AI投資に支えられたIT関連サービスが特に力強く拡大。専門・商業サービスも改善

### Page 3

金融市場概況：投資家心理が悪化し、日米とも株安・金利低下
 AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、日米株価はハイテク株を中心に下落
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機に。原油高に伴うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に
― 投資家心理が悪化するなかで、安全資産とされる日米国債に資金が流入、前月までの金利上昇基調は一巡
 ドル円は、1ドル＝150円台後半で方向感の乏しい展開
― 日米金利とも低下し、日米金利差は概ね横ばい圏で推移
― 原油高が輸入増を通じて円安要因となる一方、日米株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化に伴うユーロ安は円高要因に

米国：インフレ率は高止まり、油価再加速で当面は物価高が続く懸念
 原油高や関税の影響から物価は加速
―9月のコアCPIは前月比＋0.3％（年率＋4.1％）と8月（前月比＋0.3％、年率＋3.5％）から伸びが拡大
― コアサービスでは住居費や輸送サービスなどが押し上げに寄与。コア財については、衣料品や玩具などが上昇。関税引
き上げに伴う価格転嫁が続いている模様
 足元で原油価格が再加速していることを踏まえれば、当面は物価の上振れリスクが残存する懸念
― シカゴ連銀が公表する週次の小売物価指標は9月後半から10月初めにかけて上昇しており、物価の高止まりを示唆
3

米国：足元の消費はガソリン高や関税に伴う物価上昇で足踏みも、年末商戦は堅調な見通し
 消費は足踏み。ガソリン価格高騰や関税に伴う物価高が家計の購買力を下押し
―9月の実質小売売上高は前月比▲0.5％と減少。ガソリン小売が減少したほか、自動車や衣料品も低調に推移
 物価高は逆風も、2026年の年末商戦は堅調予想
― 米マスターカードは、実質小売売上高（除くガソリン・自動車）は前年比＋2.7％と、前年（同＋2.1％）から伸びが拡大する
と予想。雇用環境の改善などを背景に幅広い所得層で安定した消費がみられると指摘

予想
米国：雇用は減速も、AI関連需要が下支え
 9月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋2.9万人と市場予想（同＋9.0万人）を下回る結果。7月・8月も下方修正
― 民間部門が前月差＋4.6万人（8月同＋8.9万人）と増勢が鈍化したほか、政府部門が同▲1.7万人と再びマイナス転化
― 民間部門では、旺盛なAI投資需要などを背景に、製造や建設、建築・エンジニアリングなどが増加基調を維持したもの
の、情報、金融、専門サービスなどの高賃金セクターが減少
― 時間当たり賃金の伸びは前月比＋0.1％（8月同＋0.3％）と鈍化。こちらも市場予想（同＋0.3％）を下回る結果
― 総じて冴えない結果ながら、3カ月平均では＋5万人程度を維持しており、雇用は減速しつつも失速には至らず

5
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：G7は原油・ディーゼルの協調放出で合意。米国の輸出制限リスクは後退
9
 G7はIEAを通じ4カ月で1億バレルの石油備蓄放出に合意。初期20日間はディーゼルを前倒し放出
― 背景にディーゼル価格高騰。中間選挙を控えたトランプ大統領は、禁輸も辞さない姿勢を強調（米国は世界需要の4.9％
を供給）。米国からの輸出が欧州の需要を支える一方、米国よりも潤沢な在庫を保有する欧州に圧力をかけた格好
― 欧州は石油製品消費に占めるディーゼル比率が高く、価格高騰を回避する思惑も協調放出の合意を後押し
― 合意ではG7域内でのエネルギー輸出制限回避も確認され、米国によるEU向け禁輸リスクはひとまず後退

欧州：ディーゼル需給逼迫は残存。欧州のインフレ上振れ要因に
10
 ロシアのディーゼル輸出は足元で急減。中東からの輸出減、中国の輸出停止も重なり、世界需要の約6％が失われた格好
―G7の1億バレル放出で当面の供給不安は緩和するものの、構造的な供給不足の解消には至らず
―IEAも、中東からの原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品の供給制約はなお深刻と指摘。ディーゼル需給の逼迫が続け
ば、輸送・物流コストを通じたインフレ圧力が残存
 欧州では9月HICPが再加速。ディーゼル高が輸送・物流コストを通じて追加的なインフレ上振れ要因となるリスク
―9月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋3.8％（前月：同＋3.2％）と伸び加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が主因
今般の備蓄放出量と停止中のディーゼル輸出
（注） 世界需要およびロシア・中国の輸出量は2025年実績。中東は2026年8月実績を参
考に、輸出量を前年の4分の1としてシェアを概算

欧州：EU・中国はハイブリッド車の輸入制限で暫定合意。同様の措置は他の品目に広がる可能性
11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に
―EUが中国産EVに追加関税を賦課した2024年以降、追加関税の対象とならないハイブリッド車輸入が急増
― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト
 欧州委員会は、幅広い産業で貿易不均衡を是正する必要性を示唆。同様の措置が他の品目にも広がる可能性
― 欧州委員会のモニタリング対象（輸入増加と価格下落が併存する品目）は、中国からの輸入品が最多

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
13
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性

中国：7～9月期の実質GDPは＋4.5％。インフラ投資の加速で、通年の成長率目標を確保へ
 7～9月期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％。1～9月の累計で同＋4.6％と、通年目標「＋4.5～5.0％」の範囲内
―7～9月期は、純輸出が＋1.8％Ptと成長をけん引する一方、総資本形成は＋0.6％Ptまで縮小。内需の弱さ鮮明
― 中国政府は地方政府専項債の発行枠を追加（2,500億元）。インフラ投資を加速させ、成長率目標の達成を確保へ
 9月の小売は同＋1.1％と、前月（同＋0.4％）から加速。メモリー高騰が通信機器の売上額（同＋31.1％）を押し上げ
― 投資は同▲7.2％（前月：同▲10.6％） 。前年の反動増は限定的で、インフラ投資は同▲10.5％と2ケタ減が続く

― 生産は同＋5.5％（前月：同＋5.2％）。生産量では、産業用ロボット（同＋18.9％）や集積回路（同＋18.4％）が堅調

中国：政府は住宅ローンの利子補給で市場テコ入れ。人民元相場は緩やかな元高が継続
 中国政府が9/28、新規住宅ローン金利の年率1％分を補助する政策を発表。低迷が続く不動産市場をテコ入れ
― 対象物件の購入金額を150万元（3,500万円）以下に限定し、低中所得者層の住宅ローン負担を軽減する
― これにより、余剰在庫に苦しむ地方都市での実需を掘り起こし、市況のさらなる悪化に歯止めをかけたい考え
 人民元相場は、中国の貿易黒字が過去最高水準を記録する中、緩やかに元高が進行し1ドル＝6.6元台に突入
― 高水準の貿易黒字を背景とした人民元買いの実需が強いことから、当面は緩やかな元高が続く見込み
― ただ、米FRBの利上げで米中金利差が拡大しており、さらなる拡大が意識されれば元安に転じる可能性も

日本：旺盛なAI関連需要が製造業の景況感を押し上げ。利益計画は大幅上方修正
16
 大企業の業況判断は製造業が6四半期連続の改善。非製造業は悪化も一時的な悪天候が主因で変調の懸念は低い
― 業種別では、化学、石油・石炭製品、生産用機械等が改善。旺盛なAI関連需要や原油の供給不足懸念の後退等が背景

― 一方、非製造業は、長雨を受けて小売や対個人サービス、宿泊・飲食サービスが悪化
 2026年度の経常利益計画は、製造業中心に大幅上方修正。9月調査時点としては過去5年間で最高の増益計画
― とりわけ大企業・製造業では、輸出向け売上高の上方修正が顕著。上期の実績が大幅に上振れたことを反映したもの
― 世界的なAI関連需要の好調持続が見込まれるもと、下期も堅調な輸出が続けば、利益計画は一段の上方修正余地

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み

国内債券・為替：株安を背景に長期金利は低下。ドル円は150円台後半で横ばい圏
 長期金利は、株安を背景に低下。10年金利・30年金利とも、10月上旬から30bps以上の大幅低下
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機となり、安全資産とされる日本国債に資金が流入
― 先行き、株式市場の混乱が続けば、短期的には一段の金利低下余地も。もっとも、日銀は四半期に一度のペースで利上
げを続けるとみられ、混乱一巡後の円金利は、再び緩やかな上昇基調に転じる見通し
 ドル円は150円台後半で方向感に欠ける展開。先行きも、150円台を中心とした円安水準が続く見込み
― 原油高は円安要因の一方、株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化を受けたユーロ安は円高要因。目先は両者が綱引きへ

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
中東緊張暫緩，風險資產全面反攻
2026 年 5 月
4 月全球主要股市報酬全面轉正，與 3 月跌勢形成鮮明對比，AI 題材再度成為主軸，重燃市場熱
度，風險資產動能強勁回溫。以美元計價 4 月表現觀察，新興亞洲與新興市場上漲分別上漲 17.4%
與 14.5%，表現最為亮麗，主要受台灣與南韓在 AI 半導體供應鏈帶動下的強勁買盤支撐；標普 500
的 4 月漲幅也創 2020 年 11 月以來最佳月表現 。 然而 ， 拉丁美洲與東協市因與科技題材連結度較低，
使其期間漲幅相對緩和。不過，MSCI 拉丁美洲指數今年前 4 個月的漲幅仍高達 17.3%，為所有主要

股市之冠。
隨著市場風險接受度提高，全球主要債市4 月也同步反彈。新興當地債券單月上漲2.79%表現最
佳，主要因美元指數當月由約 100 降至近 98，使新興當地債換算回美元後的價格水漲船高。波動較
大的亞洲高收益債、新興美元債券、全球高收益債表現也有所表現。全球投資級公司債、全球主權債
券等債券雖亦分別上漲 1.36%、1.25%，但幅度上相對落後。
4 月市場大幅反彈的核心驅動力來自三方面：其一，投資人對中東戰事可能導致通膨上揚的疑慮
明顯鈍化；其二，AI 主軸再度成為主導行情的關鍵主題，包括 Alphabet 雲端與 AI 業務需求暢旺帶
動半導體類股大漲；其三，總經基本面韌性意外優於預期，整體勞動市場仍穩定。然而，霍爾木茲海
峽運輸尚未完全恢復，中東戰事變數仍存，地緣風險仍是市場最大不確定性來源。
根據彭博 5/4 的統計，今年 Q1 與 Q2 EPS 年增率均可望突破兩成，我們預期 2026 年標普 500
企業獲利增長動能持續 ，AI 仍是核心驅動主軸。儘管短期股市可能因中東戰事帶來通膨隱憂而波動，

但我們認為企業基本面強度足以抵銷中東衝突與政策不確定性 ，而企業強勁的獲利成長將可望為全球
股市中期走勢帶來支撐。
債市方面，受到美伊戰爭影響，4 月的 FOMC 會議會後聲明偏鷹，壓抑市場降息預期，考量後續
美伊和談時程仍有不確定性 ，預期長天期公債殖利率波動風險將加劇 。 由於目前美國中短天期公債殖
利率顯著高於過去 10 年均值，且中短期債券到期期間較短，具備資金靈活運用彈性，因此建議在債
券天期選擇上 ， 以中短天期美國公債及高評級投資級債為主 ， 並以平衡投資組合波動角度做適當配置 。

美國股市：美股盈餘增長強勁，AI 主題重獲市場青睞
4 月美股雖受伊朗戰爭不確定性干擾，在財報優異及 AI 產業高速成長帶動下，標普 500 指數續
創歷史新高。指數上揚主要由輝達、Alphabet、亞馬遜、博通、蘋果、微軟、超微、英特爾、美光
等科技股帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括埃克森美孚、雪佛龍、嬌生、默克藥廠、亞培、ServiceNow、
AT&T、洛克希德馬丁、雷神等，市場焦點已經從能源、國防回歸 AI 主題。
美國通膨墊高，但經濟表現仍具韌性，支持聯準會更長時間維持利率。4 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 持
平於 52.7，連續第四個月維持擴張區間。其中供應商交貨指數從 58.9 升至 60.6，反映伊朗戰爭與航

運通道風險升高，導致供應鏈壓力再度升溫；客戶庫存指數降至 39.1，持續偏低；新訂單指數小幅
上升至 54.1，訂單動能尚具支撐，積壓訂單由 54.4 大幅降至 51.4，新出口訂單進一步下滑至 47.9，
顯示外需偏弱；價格指數從 78.3 攀升至 84.6；就業指數降至 46.4，顯示企業尚未恢復積極擴編意願。
故製造業雖維持擴張 ， 但由於庫存已經偏低 ，若戰事持續恐將加速推升通膨。4 月份 ISM 服務業 PMI
指數由前月的 54.0 下降至 53.6，低於市場預期的 53.7，新訂單指數由前月的 60.6 下降至 53.5，低
於市場預期的 57.3，支付價格指數持平於 70.7，物價雖仍在高檔但並未惡化。同時預期新任聯準會
主席 Warsh 上台後，要等到現任主席鮑威爾完全辭去 Fed 理事職務，才會有較大的空間與明確度去
推進「相信 AI 將帶來生產力提振、進而支持降息」的政策論述，故降息時間或許延後，未來利率將
更長時間維持不變。
AI 帶動科技股財報表現優異。預估第一季標普 500 企業盈餘年增可達到 23.9%，且第二季盈餘

增長仍維持近兩成水準，科技板塊受惠於 AI，仍是帶動美股企業獲利增長主軸，但到了第二季，能
源價格維持高檔下將推升能源產業獲利，且若伊朗戰事無法落幕，預料各種原物料價格都將受影響走
高，標普 500 企業獲利成長性並不看淡。
美國金融股第一季財報普遍優於預期，對消費惡化與私募基金流動性的擔憂已經淡化。由於股票
與商品市場交投熱絡，帶動承銷與交易收入顯著成長，各大銀行放款規模持續擴張，淨利息收入成長
優於預期。消費信用方面，信用卡支出與消費貸款餘額仍具韌性，整體資產品質良好。
目前標普 500 在指數續創新高下本益比來到 26 倍、股價淨值比 5.36 倍，短期評價偏高，且通
膨風險逐漸增加、伊朗戰爭的不確定性仍在，雖美國科技龍頭在 AI 產業具備領先優勢，但短期需留
意市場過熱及估值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度謹慎，川普加徵歐洲汽車關稅
4 月歐股受能源價格大漲衝擊，雖財報優異但科技股比重相對美股較低，泛歐 600 指數緩步墊高。
指數上揚主要由艾司摩爾、西門子、西門子能源、匯豐、艾波比、英飛凌、施耐德電機、諾基亞、瑞
銀集團、諾和諾德等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括殼牌、德意志電信、阿斯特捷利康、諾華、
葛蘭素史克、聯合利華、依視路羅薩奧蒂卡、赫力昂、挪威國家石油、BP 等，市場焦點已經從能源、
國防題材回歸科技與金融股。
4 月歐元區 服務業受到物價飆漲與能源支出暴增的雙重打擊， 服務 業採購經理人指數 急挫至

47.4，創疫情後最差表現；然而製造業採購經理人指數則在新訂單改善(尤其法國)及避險備貨需求的
支撐下，逆勢攀升至 52.2，然而就業人數持續萎縮，反映企業在高通膨與需求不確定性下普遍採取
縮編策略，經濟放緩格局明顯。法國製造業 PMI 攀升至 52.8，主要得益於新訂單走高，反映需求復
甦；德國製造業 PMI 雖達 51.4，增長動能依賴企業拉升庫存，能否延續仍須觀察。
儘管歐洲央行在 4 月利率決策會議按兵不動，但此舉是反映需要更多時間凝聚升息共識，並蒐集
能源衝擊對通膨的實際影響數據 。歐洲央行總裁透露委員會內部曾認真討論升息的可能性，維持不動
的理由包括：近期數據仍接近3 月預測、貨幣政策在能源價格上漲前已處於適當位置、金融與信貸條
件已開始收緊，目前局勢「偏離」原先基準情境，通膨上行風險與經濟成長下行風險均在加劇，但歐
洲經濟尚未進入停滯。市場預期 ECB 將於 2026 年升息 2 碼，甚至不排除於年底或於明年升息第 3
碼，但考量歐洲通膨仍與伊朗戰事息息相關，若能源價格回落、需求走弱，則升息時點可能延後；若

能源價格繼續在高檔盤旋、通膨持續性跡象提早浮現，不排除6 月即會啟動第一次升息。英格蘭銀行
(英國央行)在最新利率決策會議中維持利率不變，但整體立場偏向謹慎，市場同樣預期未來半年可能
有 2 碼升息幅度。近期 ECB 與 BOE 的升息預期已帶動歐元、英鎊轉強。
川普於 5/1 宣布，由於歐盟不遵守貿易協議，將對歐盟進口汽車與卡車加徵 25%關稅(不包含英
國)。由於歐洲議會在 3/26 已有條件通過歐美貿易協議的相關立法，故川普調整汽車業關稅從 15%
到 25%，可能引發美歐貿易戰再次啟動，接下來預料歐盟祭出關稅反制措施且展開新一輪談判。
泛歐 600 指數目前本益比為 16.87，股價淨值比為 2.39，目前面臨能源價格仍在高檔、川普對歐
盟汽車業加徵關稅，短期風險增加。然而考量評價合理、伊朗戰爭持續降溫且預料美歐經過談判，終
將能達成貿易協議，歐股仍可以長線角度、作為分批佈局標的。

日本股市：日本利率按兵不動，AI 題材帶動指數創高
4 月上旬日股受地緣政治風險衝擊，所幸在 AI 題材帶動下科技股走高，日經指數更於 4 月一度
收盤站上 60,537 點，以收盤價站上 6 萬點歷史新高。市場主要由軟銀、愛德萬測試、Keyence、村
田製作所、三菱電機、三菱 UFJ 金融、東京威力科創、藤倉、鎧俠、三井住友金融等公司帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括三菱商事、豐田汽車、任天堂、三井物產、永旺、武田藥品、國際石油開發帝
石、7&I、山之內製藥、東方樂園等，市場焦點回歸 AI、國防，以及升息可能帶動的金融股。

相較於歐洲汽車出口至美國關稅再度被川普調高，目前並未傳出美國可能調高日本進口汽車關稅
的訊息，顯示高市早苗先前與川普會晤帶來實質外交成效。日本進入黃金週，但高市首相選擇飛往越
南與澳洲「拼外交」，尤其將澳洲定位為「準同盟」 ，推動以「最上級」護衛艦為基礎的共同開發與輸
出，顯然高市對於國防自主有堅定決心。
日本央行 4 月利率決策會議以 6 比 3 同意將利率維持於 0.75%，三位委員認為應將政策利率調
高至 1.0%，市場對後續升息的預期明顯升溫。日本央行同時將 FY2026 核心 CPI 預測大幅上調 0.9
個百分點至+2.8%，FY2027 上調 0.3 個百分點至+2.3%，這些預測建立在油價將從每桶約 105 美元
回落至 70～80 美元的假設之上 ， 故若油價長期維持高位 ， 通膨前景可能在7 月展望報告中再度上修 ，
而對 GDP 增長預期也小幅下修。對比會後聲明透露出來的鷹派立場，總裁植田和男會後記者會發言
立場則略顯保守，持續淡化央行升息時點，強調需要時間觀察經濟及通膨發展，且提及達成央行經濟

預估的前景下降 ， 尚無法確定需要多久來評估下次升息時點 。 鑒於過於弱勢的日圓匯率可能更進一步
推高國內通膨，日本央行仍有高機率於第三季上調政策利率，若油價再度上行，甚至不排除在 6 月就
採取行動。
截至 4 月 24 日當周，外資買超日股 8,079 億日圓，2026 年以來外資對日股的累計買超金額來
到 7.97 兆日圓，改寫今年以來累計加碼金額紀錄。近期日財長與外匯最高官員相繼釋出干預匯市警
告、加上外資持續匯入且市場對 BOJ 升息的預期，日圓貶值趨勢已扭轉。近期日圓一度單日升值 3%，
顯示 160 為日本政府當局匯價的干預底線，預料近期日圓仍將於 155 至 158 附近震盪。
2026 年以來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、高市擴大財政刺激、強化軍事安全，但本益比仍
在 19.39 倍，股價淨值比在 1.77 倍。近期日圓守住 160 關卡，匯率穩定可降低投資者不確定性，預
料日本企業股票回購仍持續推進，可望為日股在全球金融市場震盪下帶來支撐。

台灣股市：強勁經濟與產業基本面支撐下的長多格局
4 月台灣股市在 AI 超級週期與全球資金的簇擁下 ， 正式跨越了四萬點 ， 本次台股的突破並非單純
的資金行情，而是具備了極為堅實的總體經濟、強健的產業鏈獲利基本面，以及法規鬆綁帶來的籌碼
結構重塑。
台灣今年初展現了令全球市場矚目的經濟爆發力。主計總處公布的第一季 GDP 成長率概估高達
13.69%，創下近 39 年來的單季最高紀錄。這股強大動能的源頭，清晰地反映在經濟部的三月份外銷
訂單數據上， 三月單月接單金額飆升至 911.2 億美元 ， 年增率高達65.9%， 連續14 個月維持正成長。

資訊通信與電子產品年增率分別高達 120.9%與 73.7%，這顯示台灣在全球 AI 基礎設施、高效能運
算（HPC）以及雲端資料中心建設中，扮演著不可替代的樞紐角色。亮眼的總經數據，為台股的高檔
估值提供了最強而有力的下檔支撐。
中經院 4 月製造業 PMI 大幅跳升 4.9 個百分點至 60.3%，連續 7 個月呈現擴張，更是近 55 個月
以來最快的擴張速度。除了 AI 與半導體的實質需求外，中東地緣政治的緊張局勢促使製造業啟動了
「防禦性提前拉貨」 。短期內這將維持製造業的熱絡，但也必須持續監控輸入性通膨的潛在風險。
宏觀數據之外，台積電於 4 月中旬召開的法說會，無疑是穩住外資與台股軍心的定海神針。財報
顯示，第一季 EPS 達 22.08 元，毛利率以 66.2%飛越財測高標。其中高效能運算（HPC）相關營收
佔比已正式突破六成 （達 61%） ，並上修了2026 全年美元營收成長目標至 30% 以上。這場法說會
不僅確認了台積電高階製程的護城河，也確立了全球 AI 伺服器供應鏈的長線能見度。

在基本面一片大好之際，金管會放寬了主動型基金與主動式 ETF 對單一個股 10%的持股上限，
從 10%放寬至 25%。這項法規鬆綁產生了極為強大的「流動性溢價」效應。過去受限於10%緊箍咒
的機構、 基金資金 ， 如今得以大舉回補台積電 、 聯發科等核心權值股 。這讓大盤指數在衝破四萬點後，
資金高度向權值龍頭集中，成為加權指數的進一步推收力量。
綜合亮眼的總經數據、良好的企業基本面，以及籌碼向大型股集中的法規紅利，我們對台股整體
的長線投資建議維持加碼。然而，目前加權指數與季線的正乖離率已來到 18%的歷史相對高位，意
味著市場隨時可能出現技術性拉回與獲利了結的賣壓。必須強調的是，牛市中的急跌與拉回屬於 「健
康的籌碼換手」 ，並非長線趨勢的反轉。面對當前乖離頗大、但長線看多的盤勢，建議投資人不追高，
待拉回後布局，預留部分現金，保留流動性以迎接拉回時的絕佳買點。

科技類股：半導體產業持續看好，分批佈局雲端巨頭
回顧 4 月份，在地緣政治影響逐漸淡化之後，華爾街對 AI 硬體的「錯失恐懼症（FOMO） 」全面
爆發。費城半導體指數上演了史無前例的連續 18 個交易日上漲，單月狂飆近 40%。這波資金浪潮不
僅將指數推向歷史新高。支撐這波半導體狂飆的是極其扎實的財報利多，不僅英特爾靠著晶圓代工與
CPU 獲利超預期迎來大復活，記憶體族群更步入了史無前例的「超級循環」 。由於AI 伺服器對 HBM
與高階 SSD 需求爆發，導致記憶體陷入結構性大缺貨。美光（Micron）與威騰（WDC）等大廠握

有絕對的定價權，這再次確認了半導體作為「AI 算力基礎設施」的長線多頭地位。
在剛公布的財報中，四大雲端巨頭交出了令人驚豔的成績單。微軟的 AI 相關年度經常性收入大
增，Google Cloud 也寫下逾 60%的驚人成長，Meta 則透過 AI 優化廣告投放，拉升利潤率，亞馬
遜（AWS）的營收增速也創下近期新高。這些數據證明，掌握龐大生態系的巨頭，已經能將 AI 算力
成功轉化為自身業務的現金流。此外，根據 2026 Q1 財報數據，四大雲端服務供應商並未因高基期
而放慢腳步，反而持續加大投入，全年資本支出（Capex）預計由原先估計的 6,000 億美元進一步增
加至近 7,000 億美元。在這波驚人的支出中除了晶片設計商外，代工的台積電、供應HBM 的三星與
海力士，以及伺服器組裝與散熱解決方案的廠商都因此受惠，這也是硬體續漲的主要原因。
然而儘管 CSP 業者的支出續增，但投資人顯然不再全面買單，市場進入「AI 變現檢驗期」 。由於
龐大的折舊壓力與變現的陣痛 ，四大 CSP 業者在財報後的股價表現各不相同 。Google 與亞馬遜成功

用現有核心業務的強勁現金流護航 AI 支出，成為資金的避風港；反觀微軟與 Meta，因資本支出預
期大幅暴增 ， 市場擔憂長期支出深不見底 ，變現遙遙無期 ，積極的佈局反而成為短期壓抑股價的包袱 。
然而四大 CSP 的 EPS 年增率依然維持在雙位數，但評價面卻因資本開支焦慮被壓低，下檔風險相對
受控，這提供了一個逢低布局的機會。
AI 產業的長多格局不變，但在進入 5 月後，市場可能面臨傳統的「Sell in M ay」季節性逆風，
且 2026 年適逢美國期中選舉，歷史經驗顯示第二、三季往往是波動最劇烈的時期。因此，我們建議
半導體類股仍是不可或缺的持股，但也應該開始逢低布局美股七巨頭等大型科技類股。 七巨頭的預估
本益比已經回落至合理區間，且具備穩健的現金流。加上隨著 Google 的 TPU、亞馬遜的 Trainium
等自研晶片部署比例提升，CSP 業者將逐步降低對高價 GPU 的依賴，這將顯著降低長期推論成本。
因此建議適時布局相關類股，來迎接下半年變現能力轉強後的估值修復行情。

AI 電力類股：資料中心用電需求倍增，有利電力基礎建設發展
IEA(國際能源署)發布 2026 年 Key Questions on Energy and AI 報告，指出受到 AI 快速發展影
響，全球用電需求將急遽成長，有利於相關電力基礎建設發展。IEA 指出，全球資料中心用電量在 2024
至 2025 年間成長逾 15%，年增約 70 太瓦時至 485 太瓦時，占全球電力需求略超過 1.5%，未來幾
年用電需求更將進一步加速，IEA 預估全球資料中心用電將從 2024 年的 415 太瓦時倍增至 2030 年
的 950 太瓦時，屆時將占全球電力需求的 3%，AI 專用資料中心用電量更將成長三倍至 465 太瓦時。

驅動電力需求暴增的核心因素是 AI 伺服器功耗密度的指數級攀升。以 Nvidia 為例，2020 年
Ampere 架構機架功耗密度約 13 千瓦時，當前 Blackwell 架構已達 130 千瓦時，下一代 Rubin 架構
將躍升至 600 千瓦時， 屆時單一機架尖峰用電量將等同約 65 戶家庭 。在 AI 伺服器功耗上升的同時，
大型科技巨頭資本支出也持續攀升，為 AI 伺服器帶來更多需求，2025 年科技巨頭資本支出合計已超
過 4,000 億美元，2026 年預計再增 75%至 7,150 億美元，IEA 更進一步預估 2026 至 2030 年間資
料中心累計投資將達 3.9 兆美元，隨資料中心大量建置，AI 用電需求將隨之攀升。
此外，IEA 指出儘管單一 AI 任務的能耗效率正以前所未見的速度改善，單一任務功耗每年約可
降至十分之一，然而新興高耗能應用如影片生成、推理及代理式 AI 也持續推陳出新，這些新功能的
能耗可達到簡單文字生成的數百至數千倍 ，導致即便 AI 效率持續提升，整體能耗需求仍然有增無減。

在 AI 用電需求大增的同時，目前電力基礎建設供給端各環節均出現嚴重瓶頸，相關關鍵零組件
因產能不足，導致交期持續延長，包含變壓器交期平均需二至三年，燃氣輪機交期長達五年，電網併
聯在美國多數地區也須排隊長達五至十年。以德州 ERCOT 獨立電網為例，資料中心併網排隊量從
2024 年 12 月的 63 吉瓦時暴增至 2026 年 1 月逾 230 吉瓦時，但該州歷史尖峰負載僅 85 吉瓦時，
顯示電力需求遠超既有電網承載量。可預見隨電力需求持續攀升，相關電力基礎建設上中下游未來訂
單積壓量都可望保持高水位，並隨公司產能陸續開出，逐步轉換為公司營收貢獻。
展望後市，IEA 預估全球資料中心發電量至 2030 年將倍增至超過 1,000 太瓦時，其中再生能源
與天然氣各占約三分之一，分別達 360 及 340 太瓦時，核能預料將從 75 太瓦時成長至 120 太瓦時，
故各類相關零組件包含燃氣輪機、變壓器、核能及儲能等子產業都可望受惠這一 AI 用電趨勢，隨 AI
電力需求持續上升，對於各類電力上中下游供應鏈都可正向看待。

能源類股：海峽受阻導致油價居高不下；阿聯酋退出 OPEC 有利戰後供給恢復
西德州原油期貨價格 4 月再度攀升至每桶 100 美元之上，主因經荷莫茲海峽的石油出口持續受
阻，加上美伊談判持續未見明顯進展，同時期間內美國總統川普已數次拒絕伊朗所提出之協議，美國
對伊朗的封鎖行動也持續進行，故在全球石油供需缺口維持的狀況下，全球石油庫存水位不斷下降，
為石油價格帶來上行壓力。
國際能源署 4 月月報指出，受荷莫茲海峽供應中斷影響，3 月全球石油供應暴跌 1,010 萬桶/日
至每日 9,700 萬桶，其中 OPEC+產量月減 940 萬桶/日至每日 4,240 萬桶，非 OPEC+供應月減 77

萬桶/日至每日 5,470 萬桶，儘管沙烏地西岸、阿聯酋富查伊拉港及伊拉克經土耳其傑伊漢的 ITP 管
線替代路線出口量，已從戰前每日不到 400 萬桶提升至每日 720 萬桶，然而仍無法彌補荷莫茲海峽
的供給中斷，使得中東海灣國家石油出口損失合計仍高達每日 1,300 萬桶，自開戰以來，3 月石油供
給損失已超過 3.6 億桶，國際能源署更進一步預估 4 月石油供給損失將擴大至 4.4 億桶。
在供給大幅下降的同時，國際能源署也同步下修全球石油需求預估，預計 2026 年全年需求將年
減 8 萬桶/日，較上月報告下修 73 萬桶/日，其中 3 月全球需求預計將年減 80 萬桶/日，4 月進一步
擴大至年減 230 萬桶/日，合計第二季需求預估年減 150 萬桶/日，為新冠疫情以來最大單季跌幅，
下調需求預估主因荷莫茲海峽供給中斷，導致眾多仰賴中東石油進口的亞洲塑化廠面臨斷供，在缺乏
原料的狀況下被迫降低需求。
然而儘管目前荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，導致油價居高不下，然而戰後有利油價快速下行的因子也持

續在萌芽。阿聯酋 4/28 宣布退出 OPEC 及 OPEC+，為該組織近年來最重要的成員流失。阿聯酋目
前石油產能為 485 萬桶/日 ， 然而其受限於OPEC 配額 ， 實際產量僅約360 萬桶/日 ， 兩者差距達125
萬桶/日，過去幾年來阿聯酋持續因產量受限問題，而與其他成員國出現爭執，隨阿聯酋退出 OPEC，
將有利於其提升產量上限。雖然目前因荷莫茲海峽仍處封鎖狀態，阿聯酋石油出口同樣受阻，然而一
旦戰後海峽重開，阿聯酋將可不再受 OPEC 配額約束，立即釋放約 125 桶/日的增量供給，阿聯酋國
有石油公司 ADNOC 更規劃將在 2027 年將石油產量上升至 500 萬桶/日、2029 年再進一步上升至
600 萬桶/日。此外阿聯酋退出 OPEC 亦可能引發其他成員跟進 ，使得戰後油價下行風險將顯著增加。
展望後市，當前油價雖受到海峽封鎖影響，中短線上有望持續維持在 100 美元左右震盪，然而
美伊戰事未見進一步擴大，後續油價也難以再創新高，此外若未來美伊談判出現進展，海峽運量出現

緩解，阿聯酋增產也有利戰後原油供需恢復，故我們目前對於能源類股後市中性看待。

黃金類股：全球央行續買黃金，惟短線金價仍受美伊戰爭不利影響
黃金價格 4 月持續在區間內震盪，黃金類股表現也相對弱勢，主因受到美伊戰爭影響，油價上漲
帶動通膨預期攀升，使得市場對於聯準會降息預期下滑，帶動配置黃金的機會成本上升，同時避險
情緒導致美元走強，也不利於黃金等以美元計價的商品資產表現。此外，受到戰爭影響，土耳其動
用黃金儲備以支應外匯與流動性需求，短線拋售黃金也導致金價承壓。
世界黃金協會數據顯示，2026 年 3 月各國央行申報合計淨賣出 30 公噸黃金，主要就是受到土
耳其拋售 60 公噸所拖累，受此影響，合計第一季全球央行申報黃金淨購買量僅有 2 頓。然而若看加

計未申報買盤量，則是完全不同的狀況，根據世界黃金協會調查顯示，2026 年第一季全球央行黃金
購買量加計未通報買盤後，合計淨購買 244 噸，為自 2024 年第四季以來的新高，同時顯著高於過
去五年平均購買水準的 228 噸，顯示檯面下的世界央行購金趨勢仍未改變。
另一方面，在高盛 4 月 29 日發布的最新報告中，維持了對於 2026 年底黃金目標價 5,400 美
元/盎司的預測。高盛指出，目前金價主要驅動因素包括全球央行持續推進外匯儲備多元化、目前相
對偏低的投機性部位回歸常態化，以及聯準會預計在年內降息 50 個基點等三大支撐。在央行需求方
面，高盛指出，在其央行會議調查中，約 70%的受訪央行預期全球黃金儲備將繼續上升。在風險評
估方面，高盛認為短線風險偏向下行，主要因荷莫茲海峽封鎖若持續 ，可能觸發債市或股市修正，
進而引發黃金部位遭到清算， 然而中長期風險則偏向上行，因 伊朗事件與更廣泛的地緣政治發展 ，
如格陵蘭及委內瑞拉議題，可能加速資金轉向黃金配置。
另一方面，來自穩定幣發行商 Tether 的黃金買盤也值得關注。截至 2026 年 3 月底，Tether 為

支撐 USDT 儲備而持有的黃金市值上升至 198 億美元，約為 132 公噸，較 2025 年 12 月底的 126
公噸再增加約 6 公噸 ，此外 Tether 另持有 22 公噸黃金以支撐其黃金代幣 XAUT， 兩項產品合計持倉
達約 154 公噸 ， 此規模已足以排進全球前20 名黃金持有者 。JPMorgan 貴金屬研究主管 Greg Shearer
指出，Tether 的購金行為已對金價產生實質性影響，其 2025 年全年購買量僅次於波蘭央行，並超越
所有其他央行，顯示加密貨幣機構儲備已成為黃金新一個重要買盤來源
展望後市，中長線而言央行增持黃金的結構性趨勢未變，美伊衝突加上格陵蘭、委內瑞拉等地緣
政治風險，也都有望加速各國對黃金的分散配置需求 ，使得黃金有望獲得更多來自央行買盤的支撐。
然而短線上，在美伊戰爭尚未底定前，黃金機會成本上升及部分央行拋售壓力仍將壓抑短線走勢，
故預估黃金將在區間內震盪，目前對於黃金類股後市抱持中性看法。

### Page 10

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：華許入主 Fed 機率高，美債波動料加大
FOMC 在 4/29 的會議再度將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.50-3.75%。然而，理事 Miran 主
張降息一碼，而會後聲明中「傾於寬鬆」(easing bias)的措辭也遭三位地區主席(Cleveland 的
Hammack、Minneapolis 的 Kashkari 與 Dallas 的 Logan )反對 ，會議表決出現 4 票異議 ，創 FOMC
逾 30 年來委員立場最分歧的局面。前述三位地區聯儲主席反對寬鬆的措辭，導致市場對 Fed 後續降
息預期顯著弱化。

此外，川普提名的下任 Fed 主席 Kevin Warsh(華許)於 4/29 參議院銀行委員會審查通過，預計
於 5/11 當週進行全院表決。目前參議院共和黨、民主黨的議員比例為 53：47(含兩名傾向民主黨的
無黨籍議員)，由於除了 Tillis 以外的共和黨參議員均未公開就華許的資格表達反對，未來在參院全院
的表決中，華許也可望取得過半票數通過該任命案。若時程安排得宜，料可於 5/15 鮑爾主席任滿後
接掌 Fed。值得留意的是，鮑爾在 4 月的記者會表示將繼續留任理事會，這將導致未來的 FOMC 將
出現新舊主席並存的罕見現象。
綜觀華許的主要貨幣政策主張為「降息」 +「縮表」 ，兩者在貨幣政策方向呈現相反的效果，且
對不同天期的債券影響亦有差異： 「降息」對於雖使債券殖利率下修，但對短端影響較大；而「縮表」
量化緊縮(QT)雖屬貨幣緊縮政策，但堪薩斯聯儲研究(Smith & Valcarcel 2021)顯示，「縮表」推升期
限溢酬的幅度大於短期利率。兩者同時施行可能造成導致未來美債殖利率曲線淨陡峭化。話雖如此，

美國貨幣政策需要 FOMC 票委以多數決通過，前主席 Yellen 認為華許即便就任 Fed 主席，在表決中
仍難以取得足以支持其貨幣政策路線的票數。因此，即便 Warsh 上任，其政策主張(尤其在縮表的議
題上)短期將無法付緒實現，故預期在可見的未來對美國的貨幣政策走向改變有限。
然而，Fed 主席的權力在於議程設定與對外溝通，華許在聽證會上所表達的部分想法-包括降低
FOMC 會議與記者會舉辦的頻率等，將使 FOMC 決策透明度降低，或導致美國債市波動加大。其表
達改變目前聯準會青睞的通膨指標-核心 PCE 的意圖，也可能使金融市場的不確定性增加。
預期短線債市在通膨疑慮與政策不確定性的交錯下 ， 將持續面臨殖利率高檔震盪的壓力， 但考量
美伊全面開戰可能性極低，油價的通膨疑慮料隨時間鈍化，而 Warsh 立場傾向支持降息，因此就中
期來看，美債殖利率應有走降空間。同時，我們認為中短天期債相較於長天期債券殖利率相去不遠，
投資人無需承擔較高的存續期間更可享有不錯的債息收益 ， 且中短天期到期期間較短亦有資金靈活運

用的優勢，因此建議可以中短天期美國公債/投資級債為主。

一、 美國—PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和
訊號 
標普全球 (S&P Global) 公布， 2026 年 9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數 (PMI 
Composite Output Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4， 連續第四個月攀升 ， 創2021
年 7 月以來新高；其中服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)由 56.5 升至 58.7，創
59 個月高點，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 由 53.9 升至 57.0，創
2022 年 5 月以來最高水準，顯示美國企業活動持續強勁擴張。受國內市場需求穩
健帶動，製造業與服務業新訂單同步加速成長，推升整體產出增速升至逾 5 年來
最快水準，企業亦積極擴增人力，就業成長速度創 2022 年 6 月以來新高。另一
方面，積壓訂單增加及供應鏈延遲加劇，反映產能利用率持續攀升；加以近期油
價上漲推升燃料及運輸成本，使企業投入成本增幅升至 2022 年 10 月以來最高，
帶動售價漲幅回升，顯示通膨壓力有所升溫。儘管如此，企業對未來一年營運前
景仍維持正向看法，反映市場對美國經濟韌性及需求持續擴張仍具信心。 
2026 年 8 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整年化率為 68.4 萬戶 ， 較上月增加6.4%，
但較去年同期下降 2.0%，顯示新屋銷售較前月回升，惟仍低於去年同期水準。地
區別表現分歧，其中中西部及南部分別較上月增加 84.9%及 6.9%，東北部及西部
則分別減少 36.1%及 15.2%。價格方面，8 月新屋銷售中位價為 39.37 萬美元，較
上月小升 0.4%，但較去年同期下降 5.8%。供給方面，8 月底經季節性調整後待售
新屋數量為 48.3 萬戶，與上月持平，較去年同期減少 2.0%；按目前銷售速度估
算，供給月數為 8.5 個月，低於上月的 9.0 個月，與去年同期大致持平。整體而
言，8 月美國新屋銷售較前月回升，主要受中西部與南部銷售增加帶動，惟仍低
於去年同期，顯示需求動能尚未完全恢復。價格方面，中位價較去年同期下滑，
反映市場價格仍有調整壓力；同時待售新屋維持在 48 萬戶以上，供給月數仍達
8.5 個月，顯示整體供給相對充裕，後續仍須觀察銷售回升是否具持續性。

的 51.7， 月減7.0%、 年減12.7%， 為近四個月低點 ， 並較2026 年 1 月下降約 15%，
顯示消費者信心持續轉弱。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由51.9 降至 50.9，月減

1.9%、 年減15.7%； 消費者預期指數則由51.5 降至 46.3， 月減10.1%、 年減10.4%，
反映民眾對未來景氣與個人財務前景的看法明顯惡化。調查指出，消費者對高物

營受訪者普遍認為年初以來經濟前景轉弱，其中共和黨人信心較 1 月下降 20%，
民主黨人下降 13%。 通膨預期方面 ， 未來一年通膨預期由8 月的 4.0%升至 4.6%，
為今年 6 月以來最高，且高於今年 2 月的 3.4%(美伊爆發衝突之前)及 2024 年各
月水準；長期通膨預期亦由 3.3%升至 3.4%，結束連續三個月持平，並高於 2024
年 2.8%至 3.2%的區間。整體而言，9 月美國消費信心再度降溫，顯示高物價、燃
料價格與貿易不確定性持續壓抑民眾信心，後續仍須觀察通膨預期升溫是否進一
步影響消費動能。 
中國國家主席習近平於 2026 年 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪問美國，並於 9 月 24 日
在華府白宮與美國總統川普舉行峰會。會後，美中雙方未發布聯合聲明，而是分
別公布成果清單，顯示雙方在穩定雙邊關係及維持高層溝通方面取得部分共識，
包括同意建構「基於尊重、公平、對等的建設性戰略穩定關係」、推動 300 億美
元非敏感商品對等降稅安排、建立人工智慧 (AI)或「超級智慧」對話與風險溝通
機制，以及持續就稀土與關鍵礦產供應鏈議題進行磋商。此外，美方並表示中國
將於 2027 年及 2028 年分別進口至少 1,000 萬公噸美國煤炭。值得注意的是，台
灣議題並未出現在雙方公布的成果文件中；另一方面，中方則額外強調兩軍危機
溝通機制及合作搜尋失蹤美軍遺骸等事項。整體而言，本次川習會有助延續美中
對話與降低短期緊張情勢，惟雙方在關稅、科技競爭、供應鏈安全及其他戰略議
題上的結構性分歧仍未完全化解，後續談判進展及雙邊互動方向仍有待持續觀察。 
二、歐元區—9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，且為連續四個月上
升。其中服務業PMI 增 1.4 點至 53，創近 10 個月高點，主要反映 AI 與國防投資
活動增加，帶動商業服務需求，抵銷能源價格上漲對消費購買力的負面影響。製
造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7，不過，德國萊茵河水位下降，沿岸較為發達的化工、製
藥業物流成本增加，因而暫時壓抑製造業上行空間。值得注意的是，由於國際原
油價格回升，綜合投入、產出物價指數皆上升至近 4 個月最高。故儘管 9 月經濟
表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲央行的鷹派態度，使得

### Page 7

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

6

10 月升息一碼的可能性些微增加，亦強化未來升息預期，一般認為最終政策利率
(目前為 2.5%)將會落在 3%-3.25%。 
三、日本—9 月綜合 PMI 維持擴張，製造業表現仍具韌性 
 日本 9 月綜合 PMI 由 53.5 降至 52.5，製造業與服務業同步回落，經濟成長動
能較前兩個月的高檔有所降溫。製造業 PMI 下跌 0.8 點至 54.1，雖然產出、新訂
單及出口訂單增速放緩，但外部需求仍相當強勁，尤其 AI 相關需求可能持續帶
動出口表現，加上企業開始重建庫存、供應鏈瓶頸尚未完全解除，預料補庫存需
求將在未來數月繼續支撐生產成長。服務業 PMI 下降 0.9 點至 51.6，受到工資、
運輸及能源成本上升影響 ， 需求動能略有降溫 ， 但積壓訂單與出口業務有所改善 。
另，投入成本與售價漲幅均較前期放緩，顯示通膨壓力正逐步緩解，但整體物價
水準仍維持高檔。此外，企業對未來景氣的信心持續改善，未來活動指數升至七
個月高點，反映企業普遍預期經濟擴張趨勢仍可望延續。 
四、中國—美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-7 月)略增 0.1 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 1.9 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 31.1%、71.8%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增20.1%、109.9%)、化學原料
及製品(營收、利潤年增 9.1%、51%)。此外，電氣機械及器材業、汽車業營收年
增率雖分別為 11.5%、2.9%，但獲利分別年減 5.2%、16%。非金屬礦物製品業營
收、獲利下滑 9.5、46.7%，黑色金屬加工業營收、獲利分別年減 1.1%、62.4%，
反映產業持續進行減產所致。整體而言，部分產業受惠於短期原物料價格波動，
以及受到政策推動的高科技產業表現較佳，惟部分產業因陷入產能調整週期，或
政府減少支持等因素而表現較弱，產業分化明顯。 
展望未來，儘管美中雙方互相降低等值 300 億美元產品關稅，惟考量雙方每
年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程設備、電動車、
軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，因此實質效益有限。另外，雖然美中雙方同意延長貿
易休戰，但休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給予中國稀土
供應履約的壓力。後續將關注美中能否在休戰期間就開放市場、科技競爭、供應

### Page 8

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

7

鏈安全等議題取得進一步共識，若談判不如預期，美國仍可能重新祭出新的反制
措施 ， 而中國亦可能運用稀土作為談判籌碼 ， 經貿關係不確定性仍是較高 。 此外 ，
預計 10 月 8 日舉行歐中貿易會談，若談判未能取得突破，歐盟對中國的貿易限
制恐進一步升級，對中國出口形成額外壓力。 
五、印度及東南亞—亞銀上修亞洲及太平洋經濟體 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.7% 
亞 洲 開 發 銀 行 (ADB) 最 新 經 濟 展 望 (Asian Development Outlook, ADO 
September 2026 )上修 2026 年亞洲及太平洋地區經濟體 2026 年經濟成長率(GDP)
至 4.7%(7 月預估 4.5%)、2027 年為 4.5%。ADB 認為，亞洲開發中經濟體雖面臨
中東衝突延長、俄烏戰爭再升溫、能源價格高檔震盪及強烈聖嬰現象等逆風，但
在政府刺激政策、民間投資及 AI 帶動科技出口支撐下，區域經濟仍展現韌性。
通膨方面，2026 年通膨率下修至 3.9%，惟能源與糧食價格壓力仍高，2027 年通
膨預測上修至 3.3%。 
區域經濟表現方面 ， 南亞成長最強勁 ，2026 年 GDP 成長率預估上修至 6.4%，
主要受印度內需與投資帶動；東亞則受中國內需疲弱影響，成長維持平穩；東協
國家(ASEAN)上調至 4.7%， 其中越南 、 馬來西亞及泰國受惠於科技與製造業投資
而獲上修。中亞及西亞、太平洋地區則面臨能源及食品價格上漲壓力，成長預測
略為下修至 3.7%、3.0%。(詳下表) 
ADB 認為未來最大風險包括地緣政治衝突升級、聖嬰 現象造成糧食供應衝
擊，以及 AI 相關資產可能出現價格重估。整體而言，AI 驅動的科技投資仍是亞
洲經濟體最重要的成長動能來源，而能源、糧食及氣候風險將成為影響各國通膨
與信用風險的重要變數。

五、台灣—AI 浪潮推升接單動能，外銷訂單再創新高 
8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，首度突破千億美元大關，其中受惠於 ASIC
晶片需求帶動，資通產品接單翻倍、電子產品接單亦大增 83.9%，惟光學器材因
面板需求維持低檔，年減 5%。非科技產品方面，基本金屬年增 26.8%，係因銅箔
及銅箔基板受惠 AI 相關供應鏈需求及銅價續漲，加以鍍鋅鋼需求回溫；機械年
增 28.3%，係因半導體業者增加資本支出，帶動半導體設備、自動化設備需求延
續；化學品年增 15.4%，主因藥品接單成長，加上產業用化學品需求持續增加；
塑橡膠製品年增 6.7%， 主係塑膠原料受原料上漲而推升產品售價 ， 加上部分塑膠
製品訂單回溫。展望未來，經濟部統計處預估 2026 年外銷訂單有望突破 1 兆美
元，在 AI 相關需求持續擴張帶動下，科技產品表現可望維持強勁，而傳統產業
中下游產品亦可望受惠於相關供應鏈需求，惟上游原物料價格競爭激烈，後續仍
須關注第四季消費旺季是否有相關備貨需求。

CHPT
Company description
Chunghwa Precision Test Tech (CHPT), established in 2005, is a global leader
in semiconductor test interfaces. Historically focused on the smartphone AP
segment, CHPT has successfully pivoted its strategic focus towards high-
growth AI/HPC applications. Utilizing its unique "All-in-House" vertical
integration model and leading mechanical design & simulation technology,
the company specializes in probe cards, probe card PCBs, and load boards for

top-tier chipmakers. This comprehensive portfolio effectively addresses
high-frequency signal bottlenecks, providing one-stop-shop solutions that
ensure structural reliability across the global AI computing supply chain.
Investment strategy
We rate CHPT at Buy/High Risk (1H). We believe CHPT is well positioned for
the AI infrastructure cycle, with leadership supported by its unique "All-in-
House" vertical integration model and broad participation across next-gen AI

GPU and US AI ASIC projects. Its technical high ground in advanced nodes
solutions and dedicated localized support provide it a defensible position, in
our view, as advanced packaging demand expands. The company captures a
wider share of the test interface value chain across probe PCBs, load boards,
and high-end burn-in boards, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value CHPT using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings
momentum and the structural shift towards high-end testing. Our target

price of NT$4,000 is derived from applying 35x to 2027E EPS, implying the
company's average forward PE since 2024 +1 SD, which we believe is justified
given the company's entrenched position in the 2nm/3nm testing segment,
rising contribution from US AI GPU & AI ASIC projects, and the unique
competitive advantage of its "All-in-House" vertical integration model. With
these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to
value it as an AI/HPC play (rather than a consumer electronics stock), we

expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant system rates the CHPT stock as High Risk for its share price
volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target
price, and to our earnings forecasts, include: 1) margin compression from
lower-than-expected utilization rates; 2) execution delays in concentrated
US AI GPU and ASIC programs; 3) intensifying competition in testing boards

from global peers; and 4) structural gross margin dilution from a shift towards
high-end burn-in boards.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 3

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas
customers. Management emphasized that SOFC shipments will remain limited
initially as the company prioritizes product reliability and qualification.
4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

Bull/Bear: Delta Electronics (2308.TW)

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Positive news on AI ASIC
execution and project win;
reiterate OW
We expect the stock to react positively to the AI ASIC
developments; OW: 1) TPU v9 on schedule, 2) confidence in Intel
EMIB-T substrate yield improvement, and 3) second customer
win. We maintain our  PT of NT$6,188 and Top Pick.
What's new? MediaTek confirms second customer win: In our preview note,  we
had expected some discussion on a potential second customer. Today, management

confirmed the win, and indicated it is not a China CSP customer, hence, we think it
could be a US CSP  customer. We are modeling US$2.5bn revenue from the second
customer in 2028, but management thinks the demand should be higher than that.
Status of major TPU projects - on track: TPU v8 expected to contribute US$2.5bn
revenue in 4Q26 (vs. prior guidance of >US$2bn). Major AI ASIC customer’s TPU v9
is expected to tape out on time and starts mass production in 1Q28, dismissing

recent delay concerns. While TPU v9 volume highly depends on Intel EMIB-T
substrate supply, management has confidence in EMIB-T yield improvement. The
company is also working closely with the customer on the third generation
(TPUv10) and will disclose more details at the appropriate time.
Our key question to management:  Will both major  CSP customers prioritize GPU
or ASIC, if/when we see cloud capex budget shrinkage? Management responded
that, while not every ASIC in the industry can compete with GPU, the ASICs they

help to design (TPUv8 and v9) enjoy very competitive TCO (total cost of
ownership). Thus, we believe MediaTek’s AI ASIC revenue can track our forecast, if
the substrate supply is sufficient.
4Q26 revenue to grow 25-32% Q/Q, with operating margin improvement thanks
to AI ASIC: Gross margin guidance of 45% is slightly better than our 44%-45%,
given some price hikes for smartphone and smart-edge products. After deducting
the US$2.5bn revenue contribution from the AI ASIC in the quarter, the non-AI

business should be down 17% Q/Q.
We think the implied AI ASIC operating margin could be 25%-30% at scale: This
assumes AI ASIC is 35% of 4Q26 revenue and the company improves its 4Q26
operating margin to 19% (up from 16.1% in 3Q26),

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside
n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.

n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

기아 (000270)

26.3Q Review: 수익성에 대한 입증이 필요  Company Brief

26.3Q Review: 매출액 31.6조원, 영업이익 1.8조원  
기아는 26.3Q 매출액 31.6조원 (+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원 
(+24.3% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, +5.8% OPM) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 26.3Q 영업이익은 
시장 컨센서스 (2.36조원)를 22.9% 하회했다 [iM증권 추정치: 매출액 31.5조원, 
영업이익 2.3조원]. 주요 구성 요소별 내용은 아래와 같다. 
① P: 26.3Q 26.3Q 전동화 차량 판매는 30.0만대 (+47% YoY, 판매 비중 
36.2%)를 기록했는데, 이 중 HEV 17.2 만대 (+46% YoY), EV 12.3 만대 
(+75% YoY)로 구성됨. 미국 HEV 판매가 전년 동기 대비 118% 증가하며 
시장 성장률을 약 4배 상회했고, 특히 서유럽 EV 판매가 전년 대비 163% 
증가함. 단 26.3Q 중 원화가 빠르게 강세로 전환되며 원/달러, 원/유로 
평균 환율은 전분기 대비 각 5.2%, 6.2% 하락함에 따른 – 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매는 84.7만대 (+7.9% YoY) 를 기록했고, 북미, 유럽, 
인도 등 주요 시장에서 고른 성장 나타남. 26.3Q 소매 판매 또한 82.9만대 
(+6.9% YoY)로 글로벌 산업 수요 (-6% YoY)를 상회. 북미는 HEV, 내수와 
유럽은 EV, 인도는 신형 셀토스와 소넷을 중심으로 판매가 확대됨 (이하 
26.3Q 권역별 도매 판매 YoY, 국내: +2.2%, 북미: +7.6%. 유럽: +19.3%. 
인도: +38.5%). 이란 사태 장기화로 아중동 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 
28.4% 감소했으나, 수요가 견조한 중남미와 CIS로 물량을 전환하며 
감소분을 상당 부분 만회함. 연간 도매 판매 가이던스 (335만대)를 유지함. 
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 약 0.7조원으로 추정되며, 전년 동기 대비 
5,000억원 감소함 (25.2Q 0.7조원, 25.3Q 1.2조원, 25.4Q 1.0조원, 26.1Q 
0.8조원). 반면 인센티브 증가 및 가격 인하 7,270억원, 서유럽 EV 비중 
확대에 따른 믹스 악화 4,650억원, 원자재 가격 상승 영향 3,680억원 등 
총 1.6조원의 감익 요인이 발생함. 이에 26.3Q 매출원가율은 84.1%로 
전년 동기 대비 3.0%p 상승함 (인센티브 +1.9%p, 재료비 +1.2%p). 다만 
26.3Q 기말 환율 급락하며 판매보증충당부채 환입 규모가 축소되었고 
판매보증비 약 9,000억원 감소. 판매관리비율은 10.1%로 3.7%p 개선됨. 
26.3Q 기아 순이익은 1.29조원으로 전년 동기 대비 9.6% 감소했다. 원화 강세에 
따라 외환환산 관련 이익이 전년 동기 540억원 이익에서 3,718억원으로 손실 
전환됐고, 현대모비스 등 관계사 이익 감소로 지분법손익 또한 360억원 감소했기 
때문이다 (현대차 순이익 감소 논리와 동일). 1) 산업 수요 감소와 중동 판매 차질에도 
권역별 파워트레인 대응을 통해 그룹사 대비 가지고 있는 외형 성장성을 증명했으나, 
2) 유럽 EV 판매 확대 과정에서 발생한 인센티브와 믹스 악화라는 비용 부담, 원자재 
가격 상승이 관세 감소 효과를 대부분 반납했고, 3) 분기 중 급격한 원화 강세가 
더해지며 시장 기대를 크게 하회하는 수익성을 기록했다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
Buy (Maintain)

실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM) 에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27 년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35% 를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

기아 판매관리비 (좌) 판매관리비율 (우)
  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

Appendix

Document 314: Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01

Status: ok

### Page 1

October 2026
First Home
Buyer Report

### Page 2

2 © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Cotality’s
First Home Buyer
(FHB) Report has tracked
purchasing activity for
several years,
analysing
the
types of properties bought,
the prices paid, and broader
shifts in entry
-
level demand.
The latest edition updates
these measures and extends
the analysis to examine FHB
activity across different
price brackets.
Now in its fourth edition in
partnership with Westpac,
the report also draws on the

bank’s mortgage lending
data to provide additional
insight into borrower
characteristics. That
includes the average age of
FHB borrowers, a measure
not widely available
elsewhere, as well as loan
-
to
-
value ratios across new
mortgages.
For clarity, we define a first home
buyer purchase to be where no party
involved has owned property in New
Zealand before and are using a
mortgage to make the deal.
Note:
The Westpac FHB age and LVR data
are sourced from their lending records

and is not matched to the Cotality
database for this report.
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 3

3
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
FHBs’ market share remains
at record high levels.
In Q3
2026, they accounted for
29.5% of property purchases
across NZ, surpassing the
previous mark of 28.3% only
set three months earlier. The
number of deals remains at
historically high levels too,
bucking the wider trend of
slowing sales volumes for
groups such as investors and
relocating owner
-
occupiers.
This strength for FHBs is
being seen right across the

country,
with market shares
generally running above their
own long
-
term averages
(since 2005). The wider
Wellington area remains a
hotspot for FHBs, accounting
for 37% of activity so far in
2026. But FHBs have also
been at least 30% of the
market in Hamilton and
Auckland, as well as Gisborne,
Rotorua, Hastings,
Palmerston North, and
Invercargill
–
South Waikato
and Waikato District too.
This high level of activity
hasn’t meant too many
compromises either;

standalone houses have
comprised 76% of all FHB
purchases so far in 2026, up
from 75% last year, and the
highest figure since 2020
(78%). To be fair, FHBs have
had to pay a little more, with a
median price so far this year
of $715,000, but still less than
nearly $719,000 in 2022.
FHBs are more active across
the value spectrum too.
For
example, in the middle 40% of
the market by value, their
share rose from 21% in 2015 to
28% in 2025, and now about

30% so far this year.
Access to low deposit
finance under the LVR rules
remains a key support for
first home buyers.
Recent
Reserve Bank data shows that
more than half of FHB loans
are done at less than 20%
deposit (or above 80% LVR),
and Westpac’s own lending
records show a recent
average LVR for FHBs of a
touch more than 80%.
The average age for FHBs on
Westpac’s books has ticked
higher lately, from around 35
late last year to about 35.5
now.
But rather than

necessity due to affordability
pressures, this is more likely
to reflect a choice from many
buyers to just wait for the
right property without fear the
market will run away again in
the meantime.
Looking ahead, although it’s
never easy to get that first
home, conditions should
remain
favourable
for FHBs.
Mortgage rates remain lower
than at many points in the
past, KiwiSaver access for at
least part of the deposit
remains a strong support, and
the banks remain willing and

able to lend at low
deposit/high LVR too.
This is clearly a ‘good
new
s
story’ which we should never
get blasé or complacent
about,
after so many previous
years (even decades) of
intense housing affordability
pressures and a declining
home ownership rate.
As we head into November’s
election, the bottom line is
that first home buyers are
starting from a strong base.
Key findings
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 4

4
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
It’s been another solid few months
for first home buyers with their share
of house sales remaining around
record highs across the country.
That’s a stark contrast to the
softness in the broader housing
market over the past year.
Helping to support those high levels
of first home buyers have been a
couple of big trends. The first is the
large number of properties available
for sale. That’s meant those looking

to get their foot on the property
ladder have had plenty of options. As
a result, first home buyers have often
been able to get into newer and
larger standalone homes without
having to pay significantly more.
The other factor that has boosted
first home buyer numbers has been
the relatively low level of mortgage
rates in recent years. Combined with
broadly flat house prices, that’s
meant servicing a mortgage has
been much more affordable, with the

share of disposable income being
spen
t
on debt
-
servicing costs back
around pre
-
pandemic averages. For
the average first home buyer, lower
mortgage interest rates in recent
years shaved around $560 off their
monthly mortgage payments
compared to 2023 when interest
rates were at their peak. And in
Auckland, where first homes tend to
be more expensive, the saving is
around $685 a month.
While those are encouraging trends
for first home buyers, there are some

big changes in economic conditions
occurring which are likely to have
important implications for the
housing market over the year ahead.
Most notable has been the sharp rise
in inflation in the wake of the Middle
East war, with oil prices rocketing
higher since the start of this year.
That contributed to consumer prices
rising by 3.7% in the year to
September. Relatively high core
inflation levels mean we expect
inflation will push above 4% before

the end of this year and remain high
for much of 2027.
In response to those mounting
inflation pressures, and concerns
that they will persist for some time,
the RBNZ began lifting the Official
Cash Rate (OCR) back in July. We
expect further hikes in coming
months. The resulting pressure on
borrowing costs has seen mortgage
rates push higher in recent months,
with one
-
and two
-
year fixed rates
up around 0.75 percentage points
over the past year.

Even with the recent rise in
mortgage rates, the housing market
is still a lot more accessible for first
home buyers than a few years ago.
But with growing upward pressure
on interest rates, it’s a good time for
prospective first home buyers (or
anyone with a re
-
fix coming up) to
think about the best way to structure
their borrowing. That could help
insulate them from the likely trend
higher in the OCR over the next
couple of years and could save them

a lot of money over the life of their
mortgage. There’s a relatively
modest difference between short
-
and longer
-
term fixed mortgage
rates right now, which means the
cost of taking this ‘insurance’ is not
prohibitive.
Commentary from Satish Ranchhod, Westpac NZ Senior Economist

Average age of first home buyers from Westpac loans (2018 to 2026)
Providing some offset to the lift in
borrowing costs, house price
inflation is low, with effectively no
growth in average house prices for
three years now. And with mortgage
rates pushing higher, prices are
expected to remain soft for some
time. We expect that average house
prices will fall by 1.4% over 2026,
with a rise of just 1.8% in 2027.

At the same time, the supply of
housing is continuing to lift, with
more than 40,000 new dwellings
consented over the past year.  We
expect population growth to
increase, but from low levels, which
means the demand for housing might
not be shifting sharply higher any
time soon.
That combination of conditions
means that there is still likely to be a
lot of options
f
or
those looking to get
into their first home over the coming
year.

Source: Cotality, Westpac

### Page 6

6
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The national picture
Since our previous FHB Report
back in May 2026, the wider
housing market has remained
subdued and patchy
–
generally
sales volumes have been flat to
falling, while property values have
been showing a similarly soft
picture, albeit not everywhere.
Amongst the main
centres
,
Auckland and Wellington, as
examples, have been more sluggish
than say Christchurch or Dunedin.
It’s also noteworthy that the stock

of listings available for sale remains
elevated across the country.
It’s not difficult to find the reasons
why the market remains in the
doldrums.
The economic backdrop
is still uncertain, the unemployment
rate is above average, and
mortgage rates have been drifting
higher in recent months as inflation
fears remain strong. A better
balance of physical housing supply
versus our population is also
dampening prices, while the
election has been a recent reason

for caution too. It’s harder to
measure, but ‘animal spirits’ aren’t a
key factor
at the moment
either.
FHBs have gone from strength to
strength
In this purchaser
-
friendly
environment, first home buyers
have continued to take full
advantage.
The Cotality Buyer
Classification data shows that their
market share hit a new quarterly
record high of 28.3% in Q2 2026,
only to push higher still in Q3
–
reaching 29.5%. They’re also faring
well in terms of the number of deals

too, recently hovering at around
25,000 on a 12
-
month rolling basis,
back to levels last seen (for a brief
period) at the height of the post
-
COVID boom.
Clearly, lower property values
–
still down by more than 18%
nationally from the peak
–
and
improved housing affordability
(even despite some recent rises in
mortgage rates) are helping FHBs.
But those aren’t the only factors;
access to (ever
-
growing) KiwiSaver
pots for at least part of the deposit

is also a factor, alongside decent
availability of high LVR finance at
the banks.
Indeed, the 25% allowance/speed
limit to lend to owner
-
occupiers at
less than 20% equity (or >80% LVR)
is not being tested
at the moment
and recently more than half of FHB
loans have been advanced at less
than 20% deposit.
Westpac’s own
mortgage lending records show
this in a different way
–
with the
average LVR for their loans to
FHBs in recent months still
running a touch above 80%.

LVRs
tend to be higher in Auckland, but a
bit lower in Canterbury and
Wellington.
They’re also getting more for their
money
FHBs have always tended to be
willing to compromise on location
or property type to make the shift
from renting into ownership, but
lately there hasn’t been much need
to take such an approach.
Over
2026 to date (Q3), standalone
houses have comprised 76% of all
FHB purchases, up from 75% last
year, and well above 2023’s
cyclical trough of less than 70%.

For comparison, standalone houses
have accounted for around 72% of
deals across all
buyers
year to date.
N
ational
FHB activity
Source: Cotality

### Page 8

8
      © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
This shows that FHBs are getting 
the most for their money in a 
quieter market. Admittedly, the 
median price they’ve paid so far 
this year has risen to $715,000, up 
from $701,000 in 2025 
 and also  
higher than the 2023 ($695,000) 
and 2024 figures ($692,000). But 
that latest price is still around 
$4,000 lower than in 2022, yet 
FHBs are still getting ‘more house’.
Another important point to make is 
that FHBs don’t always enter at the 
bottom of the housing ladder and 
work their way up 
 – 
many 
 actually 
enter  
well above the lowest tiers of 
the market. Indeed, the FHB 
median price paid of $715,000 this 
year is below the all
 -
 buyer figure of 
$785,000, but well above the all
 -
buyer lower quartile (bottom 25%) 
of $595,000. 
This point can be made another 
way too, with Cotality figures 
showing a rising share of activity 
for FHBs right across the price 
spectrum. Perhaps most notably, 
FHBs have accounted for 30% of 
purchases in the middle 40% price 
bracket of transactions so far this 
year, up from a 28% share in 2025, 
25% in 2020, and only 21% in 2015. 
They’ve even accounted for 15% of 
activity so far this year in the top 
30% of deals split by price.
FHBs’ average age has edged 
higher again, but probably just due 
to a lack of pressure
Meanwhile, at face value, 
Westpac’s data showing that the 
average age of 
 a 
FHB has recently 
edged back up to around 2024 
levels of 35.5 again, after a trough 
of 35 late last year, could seem at 
odds with the other, more 
encouraging trends. But rather than 
being out of necessity (
 e.g.  
due to 
affordability pressures), this could 
reflect the abundance of listings 
and subdued house prices, 
meaning that 
 some/many FHBs 
might just be choosing to wait a 
bit longer to get their ideal 
property, without fear they’ll be 
left behind by a rising market.

A solid outlook
Overall, it’s never easy to buy that 
first home and FHBs certainly 
continue to face challenges. But 
their presence has remained strong 
in recent months and, although 
nothing lasts forever, it’s not hard 
to imagine that FHBs stay a solid 
presence into 2027 as well. This is 
a ‘good 
 news 
 story
 ’ which we 
should never get 
 blasé 
 or 
complacent about, after so many 
previous years (decades) of intense 
housing affordability pressures and 
a declining home ownership rate.
Purchase price paid
Source: Cotality
Cotality – Westpac 
First Home Buyer Report

The main centres
Looking at the data across
Auckland, Hamilton, Tauranga,
wider Wellington (i.e. combined
City, Lower Hutt, Upper Hutt,
Porirua), Christchurch, and
Dunedin, the national trends can
also be seen at the more granular
level
–
indeed, all of these areas
have had FHB market shares lately
that are above their own averages.
The wider Wellington area remains
a standout for FHBs, where their

share so far this year has been a
touch above 37%, well
in excess of
their long
-
term average in that
market of 30%. FHBs have also had
a market share in 2026 above 30%
in Hamilton and Auckland. The
figures have been a little lower in
Christchurch and Dunedin (both
around 28%), as well as Tauranga
(circa 25%), but in each of those
markets the figures have still been
4
-
8%
-
points above their own
averages.
Looking at the different property
types, standalone houses still

dominate in most areas, accounting
for 85
-
90% of FHB purchases in
Dunedin, Tauranga, and Hamilton
–
broadly in line with the
long term
norms in those areas. The share for
houses amongst FHB purchases is
always lower in Christchurch,
Wellington, and Auckland, but it’s
perhaps the former that stands out
most at present
–
with only 66% of
FHB purchases so far in 2026 in
Christchurch being standalone
houses, versus the historical
average of 75%. This no doubt

reflects the recent townhouse
construction upswing.
In each main
centre
, the median
FHB price is lower than the figure
for all buyers, but higher than the
lower quartile for all buyers
–
in
other words, FHBs don’t always
enter at the bottom. In terms of the
prices themselves, Auckland is the
main
centre
where FHBs pay the
most, at a median of $880,000 so
far in 2026, which is $110,000 less
than the all
-
buyer median, but
$110,000 more than the all

-
buyer
lower quartile. Median FHB prices
paid are $795,500 in Tauranga so
far in 2026, followed by Wellington
at $736,250, Hamilton $715,000,
and lower still in Christchurch and
Dunedin.

© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The main urban areas
Digging deeper into the next 12 key
centres
around New Zealand, the
story of relative strength for FHBs’
market share has remained in place
so far in 2026. Palmerston North
stands out with 33% of
transactions in the year to date
going to FHBs (6%
-
points above its
average), although Rotorua,
Gisborne, Hastings, and Invercargill
have also had FHB shares of 30%
or more this year.  Relative to their

own averages, Napier and Hastings
have been strongest, with figures
9%
-
points above normal.
But even in areas where FHBs have
traditionally found conditions
harder or have simply been less
interested, there’s still been relative
strength this year. For example,
Whangarei’s share going to FHBs
has been 25% in 2026 (versus
average since 2005 of 19%), Kapiti
Coast at 21% versus 17%, and
Queenstown
-
Lakes at 19% versus
15%.
Standalone houses tend to be the

dominant type of property amongst
the existing dwelling stock in many
provincial areas, and therefore also
account for a high share of FHB
purchases. So far this year, houses
have been at least 90% of FHB
purchases in Invercargill,
Palmerston North, Whanganui,
Rotorua, and Gisborne. It’s been
80
-
89% in Nelson, Kapiti Coast,
Hastings, Whangarei, Napier, and
New Plymouth. Queenstown is
lower at just 64%, but that’s likely
to reflect a wider range of property

types amongst the existing stock of
dwellings, as well as affordability
pressures which are probably
pushing FHBs towards lower price
brackets (and hence smaller
dwellings).
Indeed, in terms of median prices
paid by FHBs so far this year, the
highest figure (amongst the main
urban areas) has been in
Queenstown, at $1,275,000. Kapiti
Coast comes in at $700,000, with
Whangarei, Rotorua, Napier,
Hastings, New Plymouth, and
Nelson all at least $600,000. By

contrast, Invercargill ($480,000)
and Whanganui ($460,000) are at
the lower end.

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

www.dbs.com/insightsdirect/macro groupresc@dbs.com 
• Indonesia's macro narrative has 
turned more constructive in 
2H26. 
• Key domestic policy concerns 
have eased. 
• Policy alignment has improved. 
• Greater fiscal discipline should 
allow BI to remain focused on 
price stability.  
• The current account is likely to 
widen as the trade surplus 
narrows. 
• Portfolio and FDI inflows should, 
however, narrow the BOP 
shortfall. 
• Few monitorables remain, 
including factors in the 2027 
fiscal math. 
• IDR performance will remain 
heavily influenced by global 
conditions. 
 Flash

INDONESIA: Policy mix aligns

12 OCTOBER 2026 
Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.
